                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export async function getPoll(id: string): Promise<Poll | null> {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const database = await getDb()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m"  const pollRow = await database.get('SELECT * FROM polls WHERE id = ?', id)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!pollRow) return null\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m"  const optionRows = await database.all('SELECT * FROM options WHERE poll_id = ?', id)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  let totalVotes = 0\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const options = optionRows.map((row: any) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    totalVotes += row.votes\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      id: row.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      text: row.text,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      votes: row.votes\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    id: pollRow.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    question: pollRow.question,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    totalVotes,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    options\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export async function listAllPolls(): Promise<Poll[]> {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const database = await getDb()\n'[39m +
                  [32m"  const pollRows = await database.all('SELECT * FROM polls ORDER BY created_at DESC')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const polls: Poll[] = []\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  for (const p of pollRows) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    const optionRows = await database.all('SELECT * FROM options WHERE poll_id = ?', p.id)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    let totalVotes = 0\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const options = optionRows.map((row: any) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      totalVotes += row.votes\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        id: row.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        text: row.text,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        votes: row.votes\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    polls.push({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      id: p.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      question: p.question,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      totalVotes,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      options\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return polls\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export async function castVote(pollId: string, optionId: string, clientId: string): Promise<Poll> {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const database = await getDb()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Verify poll exists\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const poll = await getPoll(pollId)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!poll) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    throw new Error('POLL_NOT_FOUND')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Verify option exists\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const optionExists = poll.options.some(o => o.id === optionId)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!optionExists) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    throw new Error('OPTION_NOT_FOUND')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Run in transaction for atomicity\n'[39m +
                  [32m"  await database.run('BEGIN TRANSACTION')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  try {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Check if client already voted on this poll\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const existingVote = await database.get(\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      'SELECT 1 FROM votes WHERE poll_id = ? AND client_id = ?',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      pollId,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      clientId\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (existingVote) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      throw new Error('ALREADY_VOTED')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    \n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Record vote\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    await database.run(\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      'INSERT INTO votes (poll_id, client_id, option_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      pollId,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      clientId,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      optionId\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    \n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Increment option vote count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    await database.run(\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      'UPDATE options SET votes = votes + 1 WHERE id = ? AND poll_id = ?',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      optionId,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      pollId\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    \n'[39m +
                  [32m"    await database.run('COMMIT')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  } catch (err) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    await database.run('ROLLBACK')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    throw err\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const updatedPoll = await getPoll(pollId)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!updatedPoll) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    throw new Error('POLL_NOT_FOUND')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return updatedPoll\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export async function hasClientVoted(pollId: string, clientId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const database = await getDb()\n'[39m +

                  [32m"    'SELECT 1 FROM votes WHERE poll_id = ? AND client_id = ?',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    pollId,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    clientId\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return !!existingVote\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +

                  [32m"  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('')\n"[39m +
                  [32m"  const [options, setOptions] = useState(['', ''])\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  const handleOptionChange = (index: number, value: string) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const newOptions = [...options]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    newOptions[index] = value\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setOptions(newOptions)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const addOptionField = () => {\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const removeOptionField = (index: number) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (options.length <= 2) return\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const newOptions = options.filter((_, i) => i !== index)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setOptions(newOptions)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    if (!trimmedQuestion) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      setError('Question is required')\n"[39m +

                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    try {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const newPoll = await createPollFn({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        data: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          question: trimmedQuestion,\n'[39m +

                  [32m'        }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      \n'[39m +

                  [32m'            <input\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              type="text"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              value={question}\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  const initialData = Route.useLoaderData()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const { id } = Route.useParams()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  \n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const [poll, setPoll] = useState(initialData.poll)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const [hasVoted, setHasVoted] = useState(initialData.hasVoted)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const [voteError, setVoteError] = useState<string | null>(null)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Sync state with loader data\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  useEffect(() => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setPoll(initialData.poll)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setHasVoted(initialData.hasVoted)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }, [initialData])\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Real-time polling updates every 2 seconds\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  useEffect(() => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const interval = setInterval(async () => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      try {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        const data = await getPollFn({ data: id })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        if (data && data.poll) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          setPoll(data.poll)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          setHasVoted(data.hasVoted)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      } catch (err) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"        console.error('Failed to fetch real-time poll updates:', err)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }, 2000)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return () => clearInterval(interval)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }, [id])\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const getPercent = (votes: number, total: number) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (total === 0) return 0\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return Math.round((votes / total) * 100)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const handleVote = async (optionId: string) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setVoteError(null)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    \n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (hasVoted) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      setVoteError('You have already voted on this poll')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      return\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    try {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const res = await fetch(`/api/polls/${poll.id}/vote`, {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"        method: 'POST',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'        headers: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"          'Content-Type': 'application/json'\n"[39m +
                  [32m'        },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        body: JSON.stringify({ optionId })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      })\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const data = await res.json()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      if (res.status === 200) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        setPoll(data)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        setHasVoted(true)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      } else if (res.status === 409) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"        setVoteError(data.error || 'You have already voted on this poll')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'        setHasVoted(true) // update state to match server\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"        setVoteError(data.error || 'Failed to cast vote')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } catch (err: any) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      setVoteError(err.message || 'Network error')\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    <div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      <div style={{ marginBottom: '20px' }}>\n"[39m +
                  [32m`        <Link to="/" style={{ textDecoration: 'none', color: '#007bff' }}>&larr; Back to Polls</Link>\n`[39m +
                  [32m'      </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <h1>{poll.question}</h1>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <div \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        data-testid="total-votes" \n'[39m +
                  [32m"        style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: '20px', color: '#555' }}\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        Total Votes: {poll.totalVotes}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '15px' }}>\n"[39m +
                  [32m'        {poll.options.map((option) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          const percent = getPercent(option.votes, poll.totalVotes)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          return (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <div \n'[39m +
                  [32m'              key={option.id} \n'[39m +
                  [32m'              style={{ \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                padding: '15px', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                border: '1px solid #ccc', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                borderRadius: '8px',\n"[39m +
                  [32m"                background: '#f9f9f9',\n"[39m +
                  [32m"                position: 'relative',\n"[39m +
                  [32m"                overflow: 'hidden'\n"[39m +
                  [32m'              }}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              {/* Progress bar background */}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              <div \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                style={{ \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                  position: 'absolute', \n"[39m +
                  [32m'                  top: 0, \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  left: 0, \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  bottom: 0, \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  width: `${percent}%`, \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                  background: 'rgba(40, 167, 69, 0.1)',\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                  zIndex: 0,\n'[39m +
                  [32m"                  transition: 'width 0.5s ease-in-out'\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                }} \n'[39m +
                  [32m'              />\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m"              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', position: 'relative', zIndex: 1 }}>\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                <div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m"                  <span style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 'bold', marginRight: '15px' }}>\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                    {option.text}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  <span \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    data-testid={`count-${option.id}`} \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                    style={{ background: '#eee', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '12px', fontSize: '14px', color: '#666' }}\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                  >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    {option.votes}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m"                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '15px' }}>\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                  <span \n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    data-testid={`percent-${option.id}`} \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                    style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#28a745' }}\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                  >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    {percent}%\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  <button\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    data-testid={`vote-${option.id}`}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    onClick={() => handleVote(option.id)}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    disabled={hasVoted}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    style={{ \n'[39m +
                  [32m"                      padding: '8px 16px', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      background: hasVoted ? '#ccc' : '#28a745', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      color: 'white', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      border: 'none', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      borderRadius: '4px', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      cursor: hasVoted ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',\n"[39m +
                  [32m"                      fontWeight: 'bold'\n"[39m +
                  [32m'                    }}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    Vote\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </button>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        })}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      {voteError && (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <div \n'[39m +
                  [32m'          data-testid="vote-error" \n'[39m +
                  [32m'          style={{ \n'[39m +
                  [32m"            marginTop: '20px', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"            padding: '15px', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"            background: '#f8d7da', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"            color: '#721c24', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"            border: '1px solid #f5c6cb', \n"[39m +
                  [32m"            borderRadius: '4px',\n"[39m +
                  [32m"            fontWeight: 'bold'\n"[39m +
                  [32m'          }}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          {voteError}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      )}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    </div>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m''[39m
              },
              providerOptions: {
                vertex: {
                  thoughtSignature:
